The [Malekmakan CKD curcumin olfaction trial](Source-Text/2023-PMID36967933-Malekmakan-Curcumin-CKD-Olfaction.txt) reports double-blind placebo-controlled improvements in olfactory quality of life (QOD p = 0.045), self-assessment (p = 0.047) and categorical normosmia shift (20% vs 0%, p = 0.032) alongside an internal Table 2 pre/post arithmetic discrepancy on Iran-SIT.

The [2024 6-shogaol plus levodopa mouse study](Source-Text/2024-PMID39092515-6Shogaol-Levodopa-Mouse-Olfaction.txt) reports improved buried-food olfactory latency and non-motor behavior with isolated oral 6-shogaol in MPTP/probenecid mice without scoring dyskinesia or human clinical rigidity.

The [Han Chinese PRKN family report](Source-Text/2020-PMID33154736-PRKN-Family-Green-Tea-History.txt) records descriptive baseline green-tea intake ("Never" and "Quit") alongside onset fatigue complaints without a tea intervention or fatigue scale comparison.

The [Prottay mouse formalin study](Source-Text/2023-PMID41550165-Prottay-Sclareol-Formalin-Pain.txt) tests isolated oral sclareol from clary sage (Salvia sclarea) on paw licking and edema (with an October 2026 journal batch corrigendum, PMID:42898092) and measures zero fatigue outcomes. The [orange-odor sleep study](Source-Text/2022-PMID36229501-Orange-Odor-Sleep-Quality.txt) finds no consistent main effect of inhaled orange essential oil via nose clips in 139 healthy adults, with "fall" referring solely to falling asleep. The [Ziziphus jujuba rat memory study](Source-Text/2021-PMID34422074-Ziziphus-Jujuba-Rat-Cognition.txt) distinguishes jujube leaf extract from Phoenix dactylifera date fruit and performs no pain assay.

The [Morvaridi olive-oil versus canola-oil crossover abstract](Source-Text/2020-PMID31901082-Morvaridi-EVOO-vs-Canola-UC-Constipation.txt) reports reduced GSRS constipation after extra-virgin olive oil in 32 ulcerative colitis completers, with full between-oil tables paywalled.

The [2022 Mucuna product assays](Source-Text/2022-PMID35939305-Cohen-Mucuna-Product-Assays.txt) compared single samples from sixteen products. The [2023 Qamar correction](Source-Text/2023-PMID36737446-Qamar-Imaging-Review-Correction.txt) updates author attribution in the earlier imaging review.

The [Wenzhou subtype study](Source-Text/2020-DOI10.3389-fneur.2020.577128-Wang-Autonomic-Subtype-Outcomes.txt) reports a gastrointestinal autonomic domain. Its earlier congress report has a separate salivation item and a smaller sample.

The [COPARK cohort](Source-Text/2020-PMID32880023-Rascol-COPARK-Saliva-and-Drooling.txt) and [Figure 2 correction](Source-Text/2020-DOI10.1007-s00702-020-02264-1-COPARK-Figure-Correction.txt) separate clinical saliva categories and on/off medication ratings. [Off-period and swallowing observations](Observations/Drooling-Off-Periods-and-Swallowing.md) link patient reports and swallow-specific measures.

The [drooling chart and instrument observations](Observations/Drooling-Chart-Events-and-Scale-Validation.md) distinguish recorded worsening, drug adverse events, airway penetration and scale performance.

The [STEADY-PD III analysis](Source-Text/2022-PMID35466955-Tosin-STEADY-PD3-Emergent-Drooling.txt) tracks newly endorsed drooling. The [PDQoL7 form](Source-Text/2022-PMID35235090-Stathis-PDQoL7-Salivation-Item.txt) groups salivation with other complaints. The [period observations](Observations/Drooling-Interviews-and-Item-Emergence.md) link original participant experiences.

The [COPPADIS cohort](Source-Text/2023-PMID37065970-Santos-Garcia-COPPADIS-Drooling.txt) reports daytime drooling at three visits. The [iRBD workbook](Source-Text/2023-PMID37680305-Barone-iRBD-Drooling-Item.txt) shows how many ratings inform the follow-up item. The [paired ESSD conference and journal records](Observations/Drooling-Visit-Denominators-and-Instrument-Lineage.md) distinguish symptom ratings from swallowing residue.

The [PERSPECTIVE randomized trial](Source-Text/2024-PMID39309726-Maas-Perspective-Drooling.txt) reports separate patient-rated drooling, swallowing and speech results. The [NeuroVoz corpus](Source-Text/2024-PMID39695127-NeuroVoz-Sialorrhea-Annotation.txt) records whether sialorrhea was present. The [22q11.2 study](Source-Text/2024-PMID38661486-Fanella-Saliva-Biomarker.txt) measures biomarker concentration in saliva. [Endpoint observations](Observations/Drooling-Ratings-and-Saliva-Sampling.md)

The [oral dysbiosis study](Source-Text/2021-PMID33646178-Oral-Dysbiosis-and-Saliva-Measures.txt) measured higher drooling scores alongside lower stimulated flow. The [swallow-safety comparison](Source-Text/2022-PMID36120789-Swallow-Safety-by-Bolus-and-Diagnosis.txt) reports bolus-specific airway measures. The [Battel feasibility study](Source-Text/2022-PMID36478035-Battel-Biofeedback-and-Saliva-Outcomes.txt) separates endoscopic residue from self-reported drooling. The [CueBand registry](Source-Text/Trials/ISRCTN59284050.txt) links to its [published protocol](Source-Text/PMC9955579.txt) and gives planned drooling measures.

The [galangal membrane assay](Source-Text/2022-PMID36145715-Simon-Galangal-Membrane-Assay-Scope.txt) distinguishes greater galangal compounds from the same team's earlier ginger comparison and records the instability of three samples.

The [fruit constipation review and extraction audit](Source-Text/2022-PMID36276840-Huo-Date-Stool-Frequency-Forest-Plot.txt) trace the date estimate to the same earlier healthy-adult crossover and distinguish stool frequency from pain.

The [2024 canna resistant-starch study](Source-Text/2024-PMID39347419-Canna-Starch-Parkinson-Rat-Model.txt) measured behavior and tissue outcomes in a Parkinson model. Banana yam and ginger yam refer to the canna plant in that study.

The [Zhang 2022 journal report](Source-Text/2022-Zhang-Ginger-Evodia-Postoperative-First-Stool.txt) and [UEG poster P1009](Source-Text/2022-UEG-P1009-Ginger-Evodia-Postoperative-Recovery.txt) report postoperative bowel recovery for a topical combination. A [2023 companion abstract](Source-Text/2023-Zhang-Ginger-Evodia-Postoperative-Hormones-Abstract.txt) adds hormone and complication endpoints with likely cohort overlap. A [2021 timing comparison](Source-Text/2021-Zhang-Herbal-Hot-Pack-Postoperative-Timing-Abstract.txt) uses a separate three-arm protocol.

The conference access records cover [Japanese anatomy and physiology](Source-Text/2021-PMC8373421-Anatomy-Physiology-Meeting-Food-Scope.txt), [Indian pharmacology](Source-Text/2021-PMC9045589-Pharmacology-Prize-Papers-Food-Scope.txt), [EuGMS](Source-Text/2021-PMC8689573-Conference-Scope.txt), [SNO](Source-Text/2021-PMC8598757-SNO-Proceedings-Access-Receipt.txt) and [UEG 2022](Source-Text/2022-PMC9548091-UEG-Lemon-Balm-Blend-Members.txt). The SNO and UEG receipts preserve unresolved whole-collection screening.

The [Kampo constipation review](Source-Text/2020-PMC7381143-Kampo-Constipation-and-Formula-Scope.txt), [gastroparesis consensus](Source-Text/2021-PMC8259275-Gastroparesis-Consensus-Herbal-Scope.txt), and [GCNY laboratory report](Source-Text/2022-PMC9515651-GCNY-Dried-Ginger-Mixture-Scope.txt) distinguish compound effects, motility endpoints and preclinical outcomes. The [Ziziphus practice record](Cultural-and-Experiential/Traditional-Practices/2021-Ziziphus-Ginger-Reported-Digestive-Use.txt) preserves a separately attributed digestive-use tradition.

The [Daikenchuto placebo trial](Source-Text/2023-PMID36968570-Daikenchuto-Placebo-Constipation-and-Pain.txt) separates global response, bowel frequency, bloating and pain and records a dose discrepancy with the registry. The [NHANES inflammatory diet analysis](Source-Text/2024-PMID38455207-Diet-Inflammation-and-Constipation.txt) measures a pooled food score. The [postoperative antiemetics review](Source-Text/2020-PMID33075160-Postoperative-Antiemetics-Food-Scope.txt) separates ginger trial arms from eligible drug adverse-event comparisons. The [Barbados survey](Source-Text/2021-PMID34776949-Barbados-Botanical-Uses.txt) and [Bangladesh review](Source-Text/2021-PMID34819855-Bangladesh-Botanical-Review-Scope.txt) preserve botanical identities and the indications attached to their individual entries.

The [2023 Moroccan dried food surveillance study](Source-Text/2023-PMID37778435-Mannani-Moroccan-Dried-Fruit-Surveillance.txt) reports fig-specific detection percentages within a wider food panel. [Comparison with the earlier Moroccan studies](Observations/README.md)

The [Skoura dried fig sample survey](Source-Text/2020-Salih-Skoura-Dried-Fig-Fungal-Survey.txt) records fungal isolates and distinguishes them from measured contaminants in the earlier reference studies. [Observations and source comparisons](Observations/README.md)

The [ropinirole case](Source-Text/PMC7899248.txt) reports visual and tactile experiences. The [piribedil case](Source-Text/PMC10917070.txt) records tactile symptoms and a fixed infestation belief after drug escalation.

The [postmarketing DLB donepezil trial](Source-Text/PMC11578010.txt) found inconclusive changes in global status and the hallucination and fluctuation composite, with a favorable secondary cognitive-domain result. [Trial registration](../2015-2019/Source-Text/Trials/NCT02345213.txt).

The [2022 visual symptom source index](Observations/Visual-Symptom-Originals-and-Citation-Follow-Through-Nov19-90.md) links original articles and conference members. [Magnesium references and clinical endpoint observations](Observations/Visual-End-Points-and-Magnesium-Citation-Follow-Through.md) verify case modalities, repeated samples and separate drug and device trials.

The [2023 search-group original index](Observations/Visual-Symptom-Originals-and-Exposure-Scope-Nov19-89.md) joins clinical measurement, medication reports and congress cases. [Dried fig magnesium and visual symptom timing](Observations/Dried-Fig-Magnesium-and-Visual-Symptom-Timing.md) traces contrasting case histories and the limits of drawing a food effect from deficiency treatment.

The [visual hallucination source index](Observations/Visual-Hallucination-Source-Index-Nov19-88.md) links 67 original publications and separates conference member outcomes, diagnostic cohorts, and patient and caregiver reports. [Dried fig composition and visual symptom observations](Observations/Dried-Fig-Constituent-and-Visual-Episode-Measures.md) trace the new food chemistry and postmortem tissue sources.

Original clinical and laboratory sources distinguish visual task responses from reported events. [object contrast in Parkinson](Source-Text/2020-PMID33426159-Parkinson-Object-Contrast-and-Visual-Hallucinations.txt) · [psychosis across autopsy groups](Source-Text/2021-PMID33501939-Autopsy-Psychosis-Modalities-and-Content.txt) · [visual symptoms in LATE and frontotemporal degeneration](Source-Text/2021-PMID34597386-LATE-and-FTLD-TDP-Visual-Symptom-Profiles.txt) · [Parkinson fluctuating pain](Source-Text/2021-PMID34693041-Parkinson-Fluctuating-Pain-and-Visual-History.txt) · [Lewy body hallucination duration and frequency](Source-Text/2022-PMID35099586-Lewy-Body-Hallucination-Frequency-and-Phenomenology.txt) · [hallucination-related network measures](Source-Text/2022-PMID35262667-Lewy-Body-Hallucinations-and-Brain-Networks.txt) · [ParkWest clinical milestones](Source-Text/2022-PMID35550520-ParkWest-Parkinson-Hallucination-Milestones-and-Mortality.txt) · [resting connectivity synthesis](Source-Text/2022-PMID35853345-Neurodegeneration-Hallucination-Connectivity-Review.txt) · [non-Parkinson visual phenomenology](Source-Text/2022-PMID35876067-Visual-Experiences-in-Schizophrenia-and-PTSD.txt).

Further original comparisons cover [LRRK2 clinical and cell findings](Source-Text/2022-PMID35931783-LRRK2-G2385R-Clinical-and-Cellular-Comparisons.txt) · [MSA registry symptoms](Source-Text/2022-PMID36247899-Multiple-System-Atrophy-Registry-Visual-Symptoms.txt) · [pareidolia task thresholds](Source-Text/2022-PMID36825060-Parkinson-Noise-Pareidolia-and-Hallucination-History.txt) · [nigral pathology and clinical sex differences](Source-Text/2024-PMID38243325-Lewy-Body-Pathology-Nigral-Loss-and-Sex-Specific-Clinical-Features.txt) · [prodromal diagnostic features](Source-Text/2024-PMID38720013-Prodromal-Lewy-Body-Dementia-Differential.txt) · [frontotemporal attention and hallucinations](Source-Text/2024-PMID38725706-Frontotemporal-Dementia-Hallucination-Vulnerability.txt) · [minor and complex visual experiences](Source-Text/2024-PMID38926597-Lewy-Body-Hallucination-Phenomenology-and-Structural-MRI.txt) · [atypical parkinsonism reassessment](Source-Text/2024-PMID39113437-South-Asian-PSP-Phenotype-and-Visual-Features.txt) · [late-onset psychosis follow-up](Source-Text/2024-PMID39324394-Very-Late-Onset-Psychosis-and-Lewy-Body-Progression.txt). [Dried fig and visual biomarker observations](Observations/Dried-Fig-Extracts-and-Visual-Biomarker-Boundaries.md).

The [walnut–fig syrup comparison](Source-Text/2022-Joudikhajeh-Walnut-Fig-Syrup-Active-Comparison.txt) reports four-week outcomes against active lactulose. Its [2020 protocol](Source-Text/Trials/IRCT20200714048108N1.txt) describes two weeks of treatment. [Preparation and comparison observations](Observations/Fig-Preparations-and-Active-Constipation-Comparators.md).

The [fig leaf stress experiment](Source-Text/2024-PMID38741409-Fig-Leaf-Extract-and-Stress-Behavior.txt) and [fig peel cultivar comparison](Source-Text/2022-PMID36595373-Fig-Peel-Cultivars-and-Amyloid-Model.txt) report different animal behaviors and preparation routes.

The [date-seed rat arthritis study](Source-Text/2024-PMID38565410-Date-Seed-Arthritis-Abstract.txt) reports swelling and inflammatory outcomes at original abstract depth. Its traditional seed tea background has no firsthand pain assessment.

The [10/66 apathy analysis](Source-Text/2023-PMID37427840-Parkinsonism-Apathy-and-Caregiver-Scales.txt), [HIIT crossover](Source-Text/2023-PMID37371330-Exercise-Crossover-and-Outcome-Scales.txt), [Care4PD questionnaire](Source-Text/2023-PMID37239208-Care4PD-Bothersome-Symptom-Survey.txt), [manometry series](Source-Text/2023-PMID37476327-Manometry-and-Abdominal-Pain-Presence.txt) and [swallowing-sound report](Source-Text/2023-PMID37837871-Swallowing-Sound-Stimulation.txt) use distinct symptom measures. The [official swallowing registry](Source-Text/Trials/jRCTs062220013.txt) links the same cohort across cough, bolus and sound reports. [Endpoint observations and source locators](Observations/Parkinson-Endpoint-Definitions-Nov19-77.md).

[Espresso shared across breakfast conditions](Source-Text/2021-PMID33640376-Shared-Espresso-Breakfast-Tiredness.txt) · [PMC8446893](Source-Text/PMC8446893.txt) · [PMC10615702](Source-Text/PMC10615702.txt) · [PMC8355947](Source-Text/PMC8355947.txt) · [PMC8417778](Source-Text/PMC8417778.txt) · [PMC7281977](Source-Text/PMC7281977.txt).

[Registered fig and almond syrup comparison](Source-Text/IRCT20220406054433N1.txt) · [Fig and date flower-bud plasma experiments](Source-Text/2022-Chhoud-Fig-and-Date-Flower-Buds-in-Parkinson-Plasma.txt) · [Vinegar-soaked fig and gallstone symptoms](Source-Text/2024-PMID39799455-Vinegar-Soaked-Fig-Gallstone-Trial.txt).

[multidomain nutrition and cognition protocol](Source-Text/PMC7174118.txt) · [ACID protocol and anticipated bowel effects](Source-Text/PMC7492209.txt) · [Parkinson taste study with clove omitted from smell testing](Source-Text/PMC9171299.txt) · [Alzheimer caregiver review](Source-Text/PMC9392839.txt) · [ACID original cognition and constipation-event table](Source-Text/2022-PMID36372613-ACID-Cognition-and-Adverse-Events.txt) · [PPMI biomarker and dream-enactment context](Source-Text/PMC10627170.txt) · [whole blueberry powder and gastrointestinal symptoms registration](Source-Text/Trials/NCT04824976.txt) · [blueberry powder safety registry and two constipation reports](Source-Text/Trials/NCT05172128.txt).

The [older-adult blueberry trial](Source-Text/Trials/NCT05358210.txt) assigns dried dates to its comparison arm. The [childhood autism date registration](Source-Text/Trials/NCT04261595.txt) preserves differences in reported enrollment and allocation.

The [SAFINONMOTOR pain outcomes](Source-Text/PMC8402181.txt) use a dedicated Parkinson pain scale in one open-label cohort. The [oral rosmarinic acid mouse study](Source-Text/2022-PMID36526017-Rosmarinic-Acid-Parkinson-Model.txt) reports motor activity. [Irsenontrine results posted in 2022](../2015-2019/Source-Text/Trials/NCT03467152.txt) provide parent trial context for a later biomarker report.

The [COPD report and supplement](Source-Text/2024-PMID39181037-COPD-Corn-Soy-Capsule-Comparison.txt) identify a 50:50 soybean and corn capsule mixture. The [NUTRIMAL main report](Source-Text/2021-PMID33871558-NUTRIMAL-Gait-Balance-and-Mixed-Oil.txt), [individual-response analysis](Source-Text/2022-PMID35191215-NUTRIMAL-Individual-Response.txt) and [oxylipin report](Source-Text/2023-PMID36645979-NUTRIMAL-Oxylipins-and-Mobility.txt) share one trial with a different oil mixture. The [Weber corn-oil vehicle study](Source-Text/2021-PMID34890772-Weber-Corn-Oil-Rotarod-Vehicle.txt) separates rotarod slips from clinical falls. The [diazinon solvent paper](Source-Text/2022-PMID36916049-Tahmasebi-Corn-Oil-Solvent.txt) is an injected model. [Comparison and outcome observations](Observations/Oil-Comparators-and-Mobility-Outcomes.md).

The [ErdBEHR trial registration](Source-Text/Trials/DRKS00026998.txt) and [main paper and supplement](Source-Text/2024-Hartmann-ErdBEHR-Strawberry-and-Chair-Rise.txt) compare amounts of fresh strawberries in older adults. The chair-rise improvement was similar across groups. [Observations on mobility and the companion report](Observations/Fresh-Strawberries-and-Mobility-ErdBEHR.md).

The [potato-fiber crossover](Source-Text/2020-PMC7697495-Potato-Fiber-Crossover.txt) reports satiety and stool results. The [safflower and fish-oil lipoprotein study](Source-Text/2021-PMID33933722-Safflower-Fish-Oil-Lipoproteins.txt) and its [sex-specific analysis](Source-Text/2023-PMID37404855-Safflower-Fish-Oil-Sex-Analysis.txt) share a crossover. The [Y-maze report](Source-Text/2021-PMID34624673-High-Oleic-Sunflower-Y-Maze.txt) and [radial-maze study](Source-Text/2024-PMID38852700-High-Oleic-Sunflower-Radial-Maze.txt) distinguish high-oleic sunflower-oil diets and memory outcomes.

A [public sensory account](Cultural-and-Experiential/Conversations/2020-Earth-Clinic-Macadamia-Oil-and-Paresthesia.txt) describes perceived relief of numbness and tingling after taking unrefined macadamia oil. Its copied versions describe one person.

The [ARISTOTLE protocol](Source-Text/Trials/NCT04324749.txt) connects to the peanut-oil control spread in the main report. The [mixed-seed animal extract study](Source-Text/2020-Nandagopal-Peanut-Mixed-Seed-Extract-and-Catalepsy.txt) measures catalepsy, and the [groundnut-oil dietary comparison](Source-Text/2024-Berkoh-Groundnut-Oil-Diet-and-Fasting-Glucose.txt) measures glucose and lipids.

The [fennel accession study](Source-Text/2021-PMID33681616-Variation-Across-Fennel-Fruit-Accessions.txt) describes starting-material variation. A [fenchone toxicity comparison](Source-Text/2023-PMID36778012-Fenchone-Toxicity-Tremor-Observation.txt) separately records acute movement signs and other outcomes. [Fennel preparation and resting-tremor observations](Observations/Fennel-Preparation-and-Resting-Tremor.md).

The 2023 [oral limonin](Source-Text/2023-PMID37536186-Oral-Limonin-and-Motor-Proxy.txt) and [injected nootkatone](Source-Text/2023-PMID38001852-Nootkatone-Motor-Tests.txt) primary records describe separate animal outcomes. [Grapefruit preparation and tremor observations](Observations/Grapefruit-Preparation-and-Tremor.md).

Chicken follow-through adds [broth and cycling power](Source-Text/2021-PMID33588872-Chicken-Broth-Cycling-Extract.txt), [poultry as a modeled substitute in an older cohort](Source-Text/2021-PMID34755477-Poultry-Frailty-Extract.txt) and two reports from the [whole-chicken and exercise trial](Source-Text/2024-PMID38888088-Steamed-Chicken-Training-Extract.txt) and its [vascular analysis](Source-Text/2024-PMID39569460-Chicken-Training-Vascular-Extract.txt). [Study-family and endpoint observations](Observations/Food-Experiment-Chicken-Fatigue-Nov19-24.md).

Shared-source review adds the [veterinary P chapter](Source-Text/2020-PMC9771673-Veterinary-Food-Context-Extracts.txt), [mouse olive oil vehicle](Source-Text/2022-PMID36359351-Olive-Oil-Vehicle-Extract.txt), [biophysics proceedings](Source-Text/2023-PMC10643713-Biophysics-Food-Context-Extracts.txt), [cinnamon–turmeric cell experiment](Source-Text/2023-PMID38138438-Cinnamon-Turmeric-Cell-Extract.txt) and [FEBS poster extracts](Source-Text/2024-PMC11208286-FEBS-Food-Context-Extracts.txt). [Preparation boundaries and observations](Observations/Food-Experiment-Shared-Originals-Nov19-23.md).

- [Tanzania faith and care follow-up](Cultural-and-Experiential/Ceremonial-Accounts/2021-Tanzania-Faith-and-Care-Followup.txt), [centering prayer comparison](Source-Text/2022-Machado-Centering-Prayer-Extract.txt) and [Kenyan stigma and healing accounts](Cultural-and-Experiential/Local-Knowledge/2023-Kenya-Stigma-and-Healing-Accounts.txt).
- [Combined Kampavata case extract](Source-Text/2024-Sharma-Kampavata-Case-Extract.txt) and [practitioner context](Cultural-and-Experiential/Traditional-Practices/2024-Kampavata-Mixed-Practitioner-Course.txt).

- [Practitioner skullcap–vervain tremor-use chart](Cultural-and-Experiential/Traditional-Practices/2023-Skullcap-Vervain-Practitioner-Tremor-Use.txt).

[Mucuna personal reports](Cultural-and-Experiential/README.md) preserve both perceived benefit and no-benefit/adverse experiences. [Observations and interpretation](Observations/Cultural-and-Experiential-Tremor.md).

Source follow-through adds the [2020 aged A53T cinnamon motor experiment](Source-Text/2020-PMID32889602-Cinnamon-A53T-Motor-Extract.txt), [2022 MedCan 3 registration](Source-Text/Trials/ACTRN12622000083796.txt) and [2024 MedCan 3 protocol](Source-Text/2024-Gurgenci-MedCan3-Protocol-Extract.txt). [Observations, date correction and planned outcomes](Observations/Food-Experiment-Source-Context-Nov17-10.md).

January 1, 2020 through December 31, 2024.

The [Aran trial](Source-Text/2021-PMID33536055-Olive-Oil-Shared-Vehicle-in-Autism-Trial.txt) and its [sleep report](Source-Text/2022-PMID35884990-Autism-Sleep-and-Shared-Olive-Oil.txt) share a trial family and olive oil vehicle. The [Salvador garden survey](Cultural-and-Experiential/Local-Knowledge/2020-PMID33121514-Salvador-Gardeners-Culinary-Crops.txt) preserves grower accounts and crops. The [November 25 packet](../../experiments/1000_see_what_sticks_food/followup/structured-frailty-balance-trails-nov25/README.md) lists all additional structured originals.

The [shared originals packet](../../experiments/1000_see_what_sticks_food/followup/shared-original-scope-dedup-nov25/README.md) includes 15 structured publications in this period. The [West Bank voice-remedy survey](Cultural-and-Experiential/Traditional-Practices/2024-Jarrar-Voice-Remedies-West-Bank-Herbalists.txt) records the leading recommendations from its abstract.
